# Experiment Log

Append-only record of things we tried — probes, hypothesis tests, approaches, benchmarks — whether they worked or not. **Write the entry in the same session the result is known.** Failed and inconclusive experiments matter most: they are what stops future-us from repeating them.

Entry format:

```
### YYYY-MM-DD — short title
- **Question / hypothesis:** what we wanted to know
- **Method:** exact command, or path under scripts/experiments/, or inline snippet
- **Result:** numbers, statuses, exact error text
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** what we now believe and what not to try again
- **Links:** issue #, commit, related files
```

Reusable diagnostics live in `scripts/experiments/`. Throwaway repro code is inlined in the entry.

---

## Traps & settled questions

One line each; read this before starting on a scraper or pipeline problem.

- **`page_loader.get_url_with_reason` returns the response for ANY HTTP status, a 404 included; only `get_soup_with_reason` checks it.** A probe that treats a non-None response as "live" reports dead URLs as alive (SRC-29's first host probe: 342/342 "alive", truly 333/342 HTTP 404). Probe with `get_soup_with_reason`, the call the article crawl makes. → [2026-09-25 SRC-29 entry](#2026-09-25--src-29-inewss-dead-backlog-is-199-articles-listed-on-112-mirror-hosts-not-113-dead-hosts)
- **INEWS lists each regional article under all 112 regional subdomains; only its home host serves it** (SRC-29 #111). Dedup INEWS regional rows by the `/read/<id>/` article id, never by host. → same entry
- **Production Postgres is `newsaggregator-postgres` (`docker/docker-compose.yaml`, named volume `newsaggregator_pgdata`, since 2026-09-25; the old container and volume are removed).** Never remove that volume; the rollback is `backups/pg_dump/`. A kill signal sent from INSIDE a container does not test its restart policy (PID 1 ignores it); restart Docker Desktop (`docker desktop restart`) instead.
- **`with ThreadPoolExecutor(...) as ex: ex.submit(...).result(timeout=t)` does not time out the caller** -- the context exit calls `shutdown(wait=True)` and blocks until the worker finishes. OPS-19's per-source timeout was a no-op this way (OPS-22 #161). And not waiting is no fix when the work *writes*: an abandoned thread keeps writing next to the next one. **A timeout that must stop writing work needs a process you kill** (`newscrawler/tools/source_worker.py`, fixed 2026-09-25). → [2026-09-25 QA audit](#2026-09-25--qa-audit-of-the-2026-09-25-handoff-and-the-six-post-handoff-closures-8-new-tickets-1-reopened-1-false-mutation-claim)
- **A soft-vertical sweep that greps for vocabulary words cannot find soft leaves that contain none.** DETIK kept 25 (`f1`, `moto-gp`, `raket`, `relationship` = horoscopes, `hikmah/doa-dan-hadits`, `tempat-makan`, `wedding` …) past SRC-35/49's sweeps (SRC-50 #162). Sweep kept keys against an independent broad list, then sample titles. **Fixed 2026-09-25:** all 173 kept DETIK leaves read (3 titles each, `sample_branch_titles.py --all-kept`), 59 denied per source (`SOURCE_SOFT_LEAVES`, one set for branch + category). **A per-source leaf must be source-scoped:** a fleet-wide `sehat` would have denied KONTAN's unsampled `sehat` branch, which only a LIVE probe (`probe_branch_category_mismatch.py`) could see, since it had no stored rows.
- **OKEZONE's `news:publication_date` is the sitemap's render time, not the article's** (1 distinct value per 100-URL file); its `posted_at` has been the crawl date since June (SRC-51 #163). Check a source's publication dates against its URLs before trusting them. **Fixed in code 2026-09-25:** `OkezoneCrawler._get_timestamp` reads `/read|play|view/YYYY/MM/DD/` from the URL (all 1,486 live URLs and 2,299 stored rows carry it); stored rows repaired by `scripts/repair_posted_at_from_url.py` (owner-approved write).
- **A never-crawled source is an unmeasured source, and its first `task=sitemap` is where the vocabulary gaps are.** OPS-19 (#132) item 4 is a hard prerequisite per source, not a formality: the first crawl of six sources (+22,093 rows) immediately found SRC-49 (#160) — KONTAN listing 100 sports rows under `sportsetup`, which SYS-42''s word boundary cannot reach because `sport`+`setup` is one token. Size read-only first with `verify_branch_scope_live.py` (it predicted all seven deltas to the dedup), crawl smallest-first, and read the stored-`category` breakdown afterwards, not just the row delta. **DETIK lists 14,926 URLs in one run** — a 15-run `task=full_text` backlog against the 1,000/source queue. → [2026-09-25 OPS-19 entry](#2026-09-25--ops-19-item-4-the-first-ever-crawl-of-six-sources-and-the-two-things-it-found)
- **The branch vocabulary and the category deny-list are TWO lists.** Adding a name to `SOFT_BRANCH_WORDS` does not deny it at category level, and every source that does not override `_get_branch_name_from_url` (SRC-48 #158) stores its branch key as the category — so DETIK''s K-drama desk lands as `pop/korean-wave`, which `is_offtopic_category` (whole-value match) cannot reach with either an exact `korean-wave` entry or word-bounded `korea`. A compound name needs adding in both places.
- **Verify branch-scope work three ways; only the third finds a storage bug.** Offline tests verify the predicates, `scripts/experiments/verify_branch_scope_live.py` verifies what a real run would LIST (read-only, exit 1 on a leak), and only a real `task=sitemap` verifies what gets STORED. SRC-48 (a P1: every MEDIAINDONESIA row filed as `news`) passed the first two and was found by the third. Run one real sitemap crawl per changed source before OPS-19 schedules anything. → [2026-09-25 entry](#2026-09-25--verifying-the-branch-scope-work-three-ways-and-why-only-the-third-found-the-p1)
- **`re.sub` for extraction is a defect pattern here** — it returns its input unchanged on no match, which becomes a URL-shaped branch key (SRC-36/41/43/46), a URL-shaped `sitemaps.category` (SRC-48, 6,104 rows repaired earlier for the same shape), or a silently collapsed section (SRC-44: OKEZONE lost 38 of 72 `<loc>`). Three instances in two days in three different functions. Use `re.match` plus an explicit failure branch, and count `<loc>` entries against keys.
- **Aggregate feeds bypass the branch-level drop (SRC-47 #157 — P3, deferred by the owner 2026-09-25):** 1,598 of 22,007 listed URLs (7.3%) come from already-rejected sections through *kept* branches — DETIK 1,185, OKEZONE 101, IDXCHANNEL 100. **Do not justify fixing this on bandwidth:** the filter runs *after* the fetch and both a category deny and a probe reject end in the permanent `rejected_filter`, so a leaked URL costs one fetch either way. The real exposure is the probe's ~5% noise floor over those 1,598 — about 80 soft articles per cycle — which is why it was deferred. Revisit before OPS-19 (#132) schedules unattended runs. If it comes back, the obvious fix (match the vocabulary against listed URLs) drops 100 hard-news articles/run from IDXCHANNEL's kept `foto` desk, because its articles live at `/foto-1/foto/…` and `SOFT_BRANCH_EXCEPTIONS` matches whole keys only — prefer five per-source section extractors, as SRC-48 did for MEDIAINDONESIA. Acceptance criteria are on the ticket.

- **Test native-crash handling with `faulthandler._read_null()`, not `ctypes.string_at(0)`** -- on Windows ctypes converts the access violation into `OSError` and the process lives. And a stub-driven test of a script that writes real paths must monkeypatch those paths first: a mutant *will* run the write (one copied the live FAISS indexes into `backups/` on 2026-09-26). → [2026-09-26 SYS-52 / OPS-25..29 entry](#2026-09-26--sys-52--ops-2529-what-a-native-worker-crash-now-leaves-behind-and-the-lock-window-the-old-guard-missed)
- **A FAISS pair is committed as a whole, never file by file (SYS-56, fixed 2026-09-27).** LangChain's `FAISS.delete` renumbers `index_to_docstore_id`, so the old "a torn pair is stale but consistent" argument (DBT-10) failed once any save could delete. A save now stages the pair in `NEWS_L*.tmp`, commits it by renaming that directory to `NEWS_L*.commit`, and rolls it forward. Every load and save first finishes an interrupted commit under the save lock, and a writer tracks the generation it committed, read off the staged files, so its own interrupted commit is still its own whoever rolls it forward (SYS-60, which replaced SYS-59's owner file). `load_index` refuses a pair that does not line up (`CorruptIndexError`). Never delete a `.commit` directory by hand. Derive cleanup from the store, not from one run's events (SYS-57). → [2026-09-27 fix entry](#2026-09-27--sys-5659--ops-32--ops-33--dbt-47-the-qa-list-of-44df5b7-fixed-a-torn-pair-cannot-happen-a-killed-source-is-cleaned-up-next-run-the-worker-builds-offline)
- **`HF_HUB_OFFLINE` does not make sentence-transformers load offline on transformers 4.57.x (OPS-32).** `_patch_mistral_regex` calls `huggingface_hub.model_info()` for every tokenizer with vocab > 100k, and offline mode makes that call raise. Load from the cached snapshot directory (`core/utils/hf_cache.resolve_model`) instead. → [2026-09-27 fix entry](#2026-09-27--sys-5659--ops-32--ops-33--dbt-47-the-qa-list-of-44df5b7-fixed-a-torn-pair-cannot-happen-a-killed-source-is-cleaned-up-next-run-the-worker-builds-offline)
- **A mutation run in which every mutant dies in 3 s is a broken environment.** Run the unmutated tree first. A clone under the scratchpad that reaches the venv through a junction passes MAX_PATH in site-packages: every mutant "died" of a `FileNotFoundError` at collection. Call the real venv interpreter by its own short path. → [2026-09-27 fix entry](#2026-09-27--sys-5659--ops-32--ops-33--dbt-47-the-qa-list-of-44df5b7-fixed-a-torn-pair-cannot-happen-a-killed-source-is-cleaned-up-next-run-the-worker-builds-offline)
- **A newest-first `LIMIT 1000` queue reaches an old backlog only after the newer eligible rows drain** (QA 2026-09-28): VIVA's 5,292 June-vector rows sit at ranks 7,596+ of 28,175, so SYS-54's "close on first run" evidence is days away. Compute the rank before promising run evidence. → entry 2026-09-28 "QA audit of HANDOFF_2026-09-27_night"
- **A run lock is not a stale-index guard; the generation check is (SYS-53, fixed 2026-09-27).** OPS-26's lock refuses FAISS saves only *while held*; a writer that loaded NEWS_L1/L2 before or during the run and saved after it used to overwrite the run's vectors. Now every held index carries the generation (file id, mtime_ns, size) of the files it was read from, and `_atomic_save_local` refuses with `StaleIndexError` when the disk changed; check + swap run under a per-index OS file lock. Load a writer's index through `index_generation.load_index`/`ChunkFAISSDatasource`, never `FAISS.load_local`. A reused pid no longer holds a stale run lock (OPS-31: the lock records its owner's create_time). → [2026-09-27 fix entry](#2026-09-27--sys-53--ops-31--dbt-4346--sys-54-the-qa-list-fixed-40-of-40-mutants-killed-and-the-two-process-repro-keeps-the-runs-vector)
- **A saved-article count says nothing about the bodies; read them.** EMITENNEWS saved 935/935 bodies 3-5x over (unclosed `<p>` nested by html.parser, SRC-54 #177) while every count looked healthy. `check_crawlers.py` now reports `REPEATED` for a cascaded sample (`core.utils.utils.is_cascaded`), and samples listed entries at least 24 h old first, because EMITENNEWS lists unpublished (soft-404) posts at the head of its sitemap (OPS-30 #181). → [2026-09-26 SRC-54 entry](#2026-09-26--src-54--ops-30-emitennewss-cascade-fixed-on-four-live-pages-and-why-the-health-check-never-saw-it)
- **A 403 cluster at the END of a source's run is a rate limit, not transient noise; check where in the run the failures sit before you classify them** (QA 2026-09-29). KUMPARAN answers 403 to everything after ~100-120 fetches per run, and every URL re-fetches fine a few hours later. SYS-62 classes a repeated 403 as a strike, and the newest-first queue puts the same backlog rows in that tail on every visit, so good URLs retire (SRC-55 #201). Count failures per batch (`Processing batch` / `Status Code: 403` in the run log), not per run. -> entry 2026-09-29 "QA audit of HANDOFF_2026-09-28" **Fixed 2026-09-29 (SRC-55):** 10 × 403/429 in a row stop the source's run and leave the streak and the unreached rows untouched; KUMPARAN's limit is a ~130-request sliding window that lifts in ~1 min (paced at <0.5/s it never walls). → [2026-09-29 SRC-55 entry](#2026-09-29--src-55-kumparans-rate-limit-measured-the-403-streak-breaker-live)

- **Soft verticals are dropped at branch level by the base `Crawler` (SRC-35, 2026-09-22): `SOFT_BRANCH_WORDS` + `is_soft_branch`, word-bounded, with per-crawler `SOFT_BRANCH_EXCEPTIONS`.** **Fleet at the end of 2026-09-23: 1,092 listed → 327 soft + 140 archive dropped → 625 crawled** (it moved three times that day: 166/1,077 after SRC-35, 182/1,092 after SRC-43 made OKEZONE/DETIK/MEDIAINDONESIA legible, then SRC-45 added the media and archive rules). Before adding a name, sample 3 live titles (`scripts/experiments/sample_branch_titles.py`) -- `teknologi`, `kesehatan`, `otomotif`, `transportasi`, WARTAEKONOMI `travel` and KONTAN `english` all sampled as news/business and are deliberately kept. **A title sample is not always enough: measure the extracted body too.** SRC-45 kept `video`/`foto` on the 2026-09-22 titles, and measuring bodies split them -- IDXCHANNEL `foto` is 597/842 chars of reporting (kept, an exception) while its `video` is 205/198 chars of caption on a player (dropped). → [2026-09-22/23 Phase 1 entry](#2026-09-2223--phase-1-finished-src-35-ops-11-dbt-26-dbt-11-dbt-23-dbt-13-dbt-14)
- **A branch keyed by its URL is invisible to SRC-35's soft-vertical drop. FIXED 2026-09-23 (SRC-43): the fleet is down from 340 of 1,077 to 29 of 1,092, all VIVA (SRC-33, the one whitelist).** `probe_branch_scope.py --fail-on-url-keys` exits 2 on any untracked one, and `tests/unit/test_soft_branch_drop.py::TestFleetNeverKeysABranchByItsUrl` hands every crawler an unnameable `<loc>` offline. **Since SRC-46 (2026-09-23) that test's whitelist is empty — every registered crawler uses `re.match` + skip**, and a companion test fails if a fixed crawler is left on the whitelist. **A `re.sub` branch name has a second, silent failure mode:** matching too *little* makes several sections share one dict key and all but the last are discarded with no trace — OKEZONE lost 38 of 72 `<loc>` that way, including eleven national/regional desks (SRC-44 #154). Count `<loc>` entries against keys, and prove any `_get_branches` refactor is a no-op with a key-by-key diff. Verify a fleet claim with the shipped `is_soft_branch`, never a private regex. → [2026-09-23 SRC-43 entry](#2026-09-23--src-43-three-crawlers-keyed-branches-by-url-and-one-of-them-was-losing-38-of-72-sections)
- **The probe artifact is verified by recomputation, not by its sidecar. FIXED 2026-09-23 (DBT-35).** `TestCommittedProbeGatesRecomputed` runs the committed `.npz` over the gold `test` split under the live rule and asserts the five gates *and* equality with the sidecar to 1e-9, using the committed embedding matrix `tests/fixtures/probe_test_embeddings.npz` (keyed by encoder + gold sha256 + text sha256, ~2 s); a separate slow test re-encodes 24 rows so the matrix cannot itself become an unverified artifact. A `gate_coef *= 0.85` mutation fails exactly those two tests and nothing else. `gate_metrics` now takes `(rows, p_soft, topic_pred, tau)` — handing it a probability matrix is a `ValueError`, not a silent 0.994 recall; `assemble()` is gone. → [2026-09-23 DBT-35 entry](#2026-09-23--dbt-35-what-the-circular-gate-test-was-hiding-measured-by-putting-a-bad-artifact-in-front-of-it)
- **Opinion / analysis / editorial columns are SOFT (owner decision 2026-09-23, SYS-49) and are denied by category, not judged by the probe.** `opini`, `editorials`, `kolom-pakar`, `surat-pembaca`, `scope`, `tajuk`, `kolom` are in `SOFT_BRANCH_WORDS` and the deny-list; the rubric says so in writing and the 20 gold rows in those buckets are all `soft`. The probe **still** splits the four near-identical Iran analyses when the category is hidden (0.22 keep vs 0.44/0.45/0.55 reject) — that is the point: **when near-identical texts carry opposite labels, move the boundary, not the model.** Do not tune τ against this class. Note a rubric change also re-stratifies the gold set, because `assign_stratum` reads `category_gate` — run `label_filter_gold.py refresh` and read the strata diff before comparing gates. → [2026-09-23 SYS-49 entry](#2026-09-23--sys-49-the-opinion-boundary-and-what-a-rubric-change-costs-when-it-is-enforced-at-the-category-instead-of-the-probe)
- **The SYS-37 probe is live (2026-09-22): `INTERESTS`, the 0.35 threshold and the SYS-31 margin no longer exist; the news-worthy decision is `SemanticImportanceFilter.classify_batch` on the committed artifact in `newscrawler/core/models/` (encoder `multilingual-e5-large`, τ=0.39).** Re-train with `scripts/train_importance_probe.py` (it refuses to write below the gates − 0.02); never edit the artifact or the threshold by hand; `SOFT_THRESHOLD` is the only runtime knob. A live run on a news-heavy bucket tests precision, not recall — pick the bucket for the property you need. → [2026-09-22 SYS-37 entry](#2026-09-22--sys-37-landed-the-probe-replaces-the-cosine-score-migration-backfill-and-the-cnbc-live-run)
- **On the SYS-36 gold set the probe's five gates sit at the resolution limit of the labels** (763 single-labeller test rows, ~4% run-to-run disagreement, SE ±0.015 on a recall of 0.95 over 200 rows). e5-large × bodies is within one test row of every gate; no encoder/label/data change left is cheap. Do not re-sweep heads or thresholds — the remaining 0.004–0.01 is a gate-contract decision. → [2026-09-21 SYS-47 entry](#2026-09-21--sys-47-changing-the-probes-input-encoder--bodies-moves-every-gate-to-its-floor-the-last-000400-1-is-below-what-the-labels-can-resolve)
- **A linear (or MLP) probe on the MiniLM embedding cannot meet SYS-37's gates on the 2026-09-21 gold set** — 30 configurations sit on one curve: mixed-bucket precision 0.71 at finance recall 0.95, 0.90 precision only at recall ≈ 0.63; topic accuracy caps at 0.65 (labels agree at 0.71). The lever is the input (encoder, bodies, gate contract), not the head. → [2026-09-21 SYS-37 entry](#2026-09-21--sys-37-the-linear-probe-on-the-minilm-embedding-is-below-every-precisiontopic-gate-and-the-gates-conflict--stopped-not-shipped)
- **Before re-drawing a labelled sample, check that its strata still exist in the DB.** SYS-36's `finance_reject` (finance + `rejected_filter` + no body) and `soft_save` populations are both 0 rows after SYS-35's re-queue and SYS-34's cleanup; a fresh `sample` silently drops them. Metadata drift (a category rewrite) is `label_filter_gold.py refresh` on the frozen file, never a re-draw. → [2026-09-21 Phase 0.5 entry](#2026-09-21--phase-05-sys-42-sys-43sys-44-dbt-32-worked-sys-45-proposed-on-sys-37)
- **A deny-list *substring* stem is a collision waiting for the next source's vocabulary -- word-bound it or don't add it.** `sport` matched `transportasi` (2026-09-19), and the `celeb` stem added by SYS-34 on 2026-09-20 matches INEWS's Sulawesi desk `celebes` (200 hard-news rows, `gate=deny`). Before adding a stem, run it against every live `(source, category)` (the gate-rule audit in the 2026-09-21 QA entry takes 5 s) and add the collision to the test. → SYS-42 (#137)
- **A committed fixture that carries DB metadata (`category`, `stratum`) goes stale the moment a repair script rewrites that metadata -- refresh it, don't trust the freeze date.** The SYS-36 gold sample was frozen 40 minutes before `fix_url_shaped_categories.py` rewrote 6,786 categories; 336 gold rows still carry URL categories and 178 hard-news rows sit in `mixed_bucket`. → SYS-44 (#139)
- **Stratifying by `(source, category, status)` cell on a corpus with syndicated mirror hosts draws the same article once per host.** INEWS's 126 regional "categories" put 319 rows / 164 distinct headlines into the gold set, 46 test rows leak from train and 8 headlines carry both labels. Dedup the population by normalised headline first; assert uniqueness on the output. → SYS-43 (#138)
- **"100% saved" after a re-queue is a recall number; read the precision side before calling the gate right.** The SYS-35 allow set is 85.7% news on the gold set (ANTARANEWS `Ekonomi` 33%, SINDONEWS `ekbis` 53%); the re-queued population was 20% soft and every row of it is now `saved`. → SYS-45 (#140)
- **Narrowing `_get_branches` to "the finance leaf" is not done until you have listed every finance-looking `<sitemap>` in the live index and decided about each one.** INEWS has three disjoint finance sub-sections (`finance/keuangan`, `finance/makro`, `finance/bisnis`; SRC-27 kept one on the false premise there was only one) and LIPUTAN6 has `saham` next to `bisnis` (SRC-05 kept one). Grep the live index for `ekonomi|bisnis|finan|keuangan|uang|saham|bursa|invest|makro|business|econ` and compare with `_get_branches(soup)` before closing any SRC branch ticket. → SRC-30 (#112) · SRC-31 (#113) · [2026-09-20 QA audit](#2026-09-20--qa-audit-of-the-src-27src-28dbt-29dbt-30-closures-with-a-fresh-eyes-pass-over-the-whole-2026-09-1920-batch-6-new-tickets)
- ~~SYS-31's category deny-list is allow-by-default and only knows the vocabulary that was in the DB on 2026-09-19.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-20 (SYS-34, #115, code; cleanup pending owner):** the IDNTIMES vocabulary and every other soft name in the DB was sampled (3-4 random titles each) and added; `kriminal`/`edukasi` left (news); the service now WARNs the first time a source saves under a (source, category) pair it never saved under before, so the *next* vocabulary is seen on day one. **A category name is not evidence in either direction**: OKEZONE `muslim` (haji security, Wamenhaj) and `ototekno` (Meta subscriptions) look soft and are news; JPNN `donwori` looks like nothing and is a relationship-confession column with 24 saves. Sample before listing. → [2026-09-20 Phase 0 entry](#2026-09-20--phase-0-of-the-news-worthy-programme-sys-22-src-11-src-36src-41-sys-35sys-34-sys-33-sys-36)
- ~~SYS-32's URL-slug headline is a permanent placeholder, not a stopgap.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-20 (SYS-33, #114, code; 486-row repair pending owner):** `Crawler._is_placeholder_headline` (headline == slug-derived text AND the slug ends in an id token) makes `_fetch_news_details` take og:title, and the service writes it back to `sitemaps.headline` for every fetched page, rejected ones included. **Equality with the slug alone is not the test**: 3,580 genuine title-cased, punctuation-free headlines on MEDIAINDONESIA/JPNN/INEWS/KOMPAS coincide with their own slug ("Keadilan Terkubur Longsor"); the id tail (`-hCa5`, `-2287141`) is what distinguishes the placeholder. → same entry
- **`backfill_sitemap_crawl_status.py` still applies only half of SYS-31** (category gate yes, margin check no -- DBT-29 ported one of the two); 35.9% of the rows it leaves `pending` are ones the live filter rejects. Its docstring says "Same decision as `is_important()`"; it is not, until DBT-31 lands. → DBT-31 (#116) · same entry
- **SRC-29's dead-backlog shape is INEWS-only, measured 2026-09-20:** 25 random eligible-backlog URLs per source on the 13 largest queues (VIVA, MEDIAINDONESIA, JPNN, ...) all answer `200` at 76-100%; the non-200s are timeouts/rate limits, not 404/410. Don't re-probe for this without a new reason. → same entry
- ~~The semantic filter's raw 0.35 threshold rejects 30-55% of every outlet's dedicated finance section, permanently~~ (world oil prices 0.254, Boeing shares 0.203, BI/QRIS 0.288). **Gate fixed 2026-09-20 (SYS-35, #118, code; 2,080-row re-queue pending owner):** `category_gate()` is symmetric -- a news desk (`NEWS_CATEGORIES`) is kept before the score, exactly as a soft vertical is rejected before it; only mixed buckets are scored. The score itself is untouched and still ~50% recall on finance text: the per-run `semantic-score recall on news categories` INFO line measures it until SYS-37 replaces it. **`news`/`berita`/`news-sitemap` are not on the allow-set (owner decision 2026-09-20, on SYS-35)** -- KUMPARAN `news` sampled 1-of-4 hard news (gallery, how-to, opinion beside it), VIVA `news-sitemap` 0-of-4; those are the buckets SYS-37's precision gate is measured on. → [2026-09-20 Phase 0 entry](#2026-09-20--phase-0-of-the-news-worthy-programme-sys-22-src-11-src-36src-41-sys-35sys-34-sys-33-sys-36) · [2026-09-20 VIVA audit](#2026-09-20--full-audit-of-viva-what-is-actually-in-the-39k-backlog-what-a-real-run-would-save-and-why-the-finance-section-itself-loses-42)
- **`re.sub` on a non-matching link returns its input: four crawlers have keyed branches by their full URL this way** (IDNTIMES SRC-14, VIVA SRC-33, CNN SRC-36, JPNN SRC-41 -- the last one was in the 09-20 fleet probe's `URL-KEYS` flag and nobody ticketed it). Use `re.match` + skip, expose one `branch_name_from_url(link)` per crawler, and repair stored rows through that same function (`scripts/fix_url_shaped_categories.py`), never a regex re-derived in the script. A regional-network key must carry the host (`bali/politik`): fifteen `politik` leaves collapse to one dict entry otherwise. → same entry
- **Do not run `task=sitemap`/`task=all` for VIVA until SRC-33 lands.** `_get_branches` returns 53 branches (24 `www` sections + 29 subdomains keyed by their full URL), the 39k backlog is 97.8% non-finance, and the newest-1,000 `full_text` window has zero `bisnis` rows. Before the first real run on any big untested backlog, do the dry run shape from the VIVA entry (real `batch_crawling_details` + real filter, nothing saved) -- it answers "what would this save" for ~200 fetches instead of ~130 junk production rows. → SRC-33 (#119) · same entry
- **`scripts/check_crawlers.py` never calls `save_sitemaps`/`save_newsdetails`/`save_chunk_data` — it is a read-only extraction sampler, not a save-path test.** "Verified with `check_crawlers.py`" only proves fetch+extraction works; a fix to sitemap parsing, dedup, or DTO construction (SYS-32's headline/timestamp fallback, SRC-16's two-level traversal) still needs one real `task=sitemap` run (cheap — no full-text fetch, no embedding) before it's actually proven end to end. → [2026-09-20 remediation-batch QA audit](#2026-09-20--qa-audit-of-the-2026-09-1920-remediation-batch-does-it-all-actually-hold)
- **A `Fixes #N` commit footer only auto-closes the issue if it lands on (or is merged to) `main` — `dev` never triggers it.** SYS-14 (#33) and DBT-07 (#34) were fixed and committed with proper footers on 2026-09-16 but stayed open on GitHub for two days since `dev` has never merged to `main`. Check `git log --oneline --all | grep <TICKET>` before assuming an open ticket needs new work. → [2026-09-18 P2-medium sweep](#2026-09-18--p2-medium-sweep-9-tickets-fixed-2-more-filed-and-fixed-along-the-way-ops-04-built-and-dbt-20-partially-resolved)
- **BISNIS's 403 is a genuine Cloudflare managed challenge (`Cf-Mitigated: challenge`, `<title>Just a moment...</title>`), confirmed 2026-09-19 across every path/subdomain tried** (`/sitemap-news.xml`, `robots.txt`'s `/sitemap.xml`, `/rss`, `finansial.bisnis.com`) -- not a header/UA gap. Modern headers (Chrome UA, `Accept-Language: id-ID`, `Referer`) made no difference, as expected for a JS challenge. Fixing this needs a real headless browser, and Cloudflare's modern challenges routinely fingerprint headless automation too -- success isn't guaranteed even with that investment. Don't re-try header tweaks; the next attempt should start from `core/page_loader/headless_page_loader.py` (currently unconfigured for Windows -- `EXECUTABLE_PATH` defaults to a macOS path) or accept BISNIS as unfixable without it. → SRC-03 (#14)
- **Changing the User-Agent does not fix any current sitemap failure.** All 32 sitemap URLs return the same status with the 2017 default UA and a modern Chrome UA. 403s (BISNIS, IDNTIMES) are server-side bot protection, 404s are dead URLs. → [2026-09-15 UA probe](#2026-09-15--user-agent-is-not-why-sitemaps-fail)
- **A "BROKEN" scraper is usually not a layout change.** Of 14 unhealthy outlets on 2026-09-15, only 2 were article-layout changes; 5 were dead URLs, 2 were 403s, 1 TLS, 3 sitemap-format changes, 1 crawler crash. Check the sitemap URL and index format first. → [2026-09-15 health check](#2026-09-15--full-health-check-of-all-32-crawlers)
- ~~LIPUTAN6's sitemap index format changed — `_get_branches` finds nothing.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (SRC-05, #16):** the live index moved from `<section>/sitemap_news.xml` to `<section>/sitemap.xml`; `_get_branches` now matches the new pattern and keeps `bisnis`. Also found while verifying: liputan6.com's article pages 404 with `?page=all` appended (the base `Crawler._get_link` appends it to every URL except JPNN) -- overrode `_get_link` for this outlet too, or the branch fix alone would have looked like "listing OK, extraction 0/2" (`PARTIAL`) instead of `OK`. And `_get_reporter_from_text` was a bare `pass` (implicit `None`) -- fixed as SRC-25 (#105), same shape as SRC-21. **A branch-parsing fix is not "done" until `check_crawlers.py` shows OK, not just non-zero links -- the link-fetch step can still be broken separately.**
- ~~"Dead sitemap URL" is not the diagnosis until you have read `_get_branches`.~~ ANTARANEWS and SUARA never parsed the index — their branch lists were hardcoded in `antaranews_branch.py` / `suara_branch.py` and the enum URL was only a liveness probe. **Both fixed 2026-09-19: SRC-13 (#91)** -- `URL.ANTARANEWS` now points at a live host (`rss/ekonomi.xml`), `_get_branches` restricted to the 4 `ekonomi*` feeds (55 of the network's 60 feeds are off-topic per SYS-31), and the category label fixed (was the constant string `"ANTARA News"` for every branch, regardless of which of the 4 feeds it came from). **SRC-12 (#90)** -- see the SUARA entry above. Check the newest listed date, not the count, remains true generally. → [2026-09-19 SRC-13 fix](#2026-09-19--src-13-fix-do-the-4-ekonomi-feeds-and-the-category-fix-actually-work) · OPS-17 (#89)
- ~~TIRTO, TEMPO and BATAMPOS (Yoast) serve `lastmod`-only sitemaps — no `news:title`, no `news:publication_date`.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (SYS-32, #94):** `Crawler._get_timestamp` falls back to `<lastmod>`; `_get_sitemap` derives a placeholder headline from the URL slug when there's no `news:title` (SitemapDTO.headline is pydantic-validated `str` — a bare `None` was never going to reach `save_sitemaps` as a `NotNullViolation`, it fails DTO construction inside `_get_sitemap`'s own try/except first, so the real prior behavior was "silently dropped at INFO", not the DB exception the ticket described); an entry with neither date source is dropped up front with one `WARNING` per branch (`SitemapScrapeBatch.skipped_no_date`, logged by `crawl_sitemaps` next to `new_count`) instead of N per-row log lines. `_get_news_details` prefers a real `og:title`/`<title>` over an empty sitemap headline. Live-verified against TIRTO's real `sitemap/ekonomi/sitemap-ekonomi-post-september-2026.xml`: 275/275 entries now build a valid `SitemapDTO` with a WIB timestamp and a readable (if slug-derived) headline, 0 skipped. → [2026-09-19 SYS-32 fix](#2026-09-19--sys-32-fix-does-a-lastmodplaceholder-headline-fallback-actually-save-tirtos-rows) · SRC-16 (#95) still needs its own `_get_branches` rewrite before TIRTO's sitemap crawl reaches this leaf at all
- ~~INEWS's TLS failure is the venv's `certifi==2022.12.7` (no Sectigo R46 root), not the site.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (DBT-28, #98; closed SRC-04, #15):** `pip install -U certifi` (2022.12.7 -> 2026.7.22), pin updated in `requirements.txt`. No code change, no `verify=False`. `check_crawlers.py --website INEWS` now fetches the sitemap (200) but reports `FALLBACK` (site extractor dead, rescued by trafilatura) -- a separate pre-existing issue, filed as SRC-19 (#99). Never reach for `verify=False`; check the bundle first. → same entry
- ~~KAPANLAGI has no economy section and BATAMPOS no usable sitemap — retire them rather than fix them.~~ **Retired 2026-09-20 (SRC-17/SRC-18, #96/#97; user decision):** removed from `WebsiteName`, `URL`, `CRAWLER_DICT`, `WEB_URL_DICT`; `newscrawler/infrastructure/datasource/scrapers/{kapanlagi,batampos}/` deleted; `__main__.py`'s `HEALTHY` list updated to include every outlet fixed 2026-09-19/20 (ANTARANEWS, TIRTO, SUARA, LIPUTAN6, MERDEKA, TEMPO, IDNTIMES, INEWS). Pipeline is now ~30 outlets, not 32. ~~IDNTIMES is no longer 403 at the root (`/sitemap.xml` is 200, only the old path is blocked).~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (SRC-14, #92):** `URL.IDNTIMES` -> `/sitemap.xml`; `_get_branches`' `re.sub` used to return a non-matching link unchanged, so every `sitemap-web.xml`/`sitemap-image.xml` entry became a branch keyed by its own full URL -- now `re.match` skips anything that isn't `<section>/sitemap-news.xml`. 16 clean branches incl. `business`, 0 full-URL keys. - ~~SUARA's 20 hardcoded regional sitemaps are frozen at 2022-10-23.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (SRC-12, #90):** `URL.SUARA` now points at the real index (`https://www.suara.com/sitemap.xml`), `_get_branches` parses it and keeps `bisnis`'s `sitemap-news.xml` leaf, `SuaraNetwork`/`suara_branch.py` deleted, the `category=None` special-case in `Crawler._get_sitemap` removed (category is now a real section name). **`SuaraCrawler.__init__` had never set `self.website_url` at all** -- `_get_branches` used to ignore the fetched index completely, so nothing ever needed it; `check_crawlers.py` caught this immediately as `get_soup(None)` before the fix was even complete. - ~~"OK" in `check_crawlers.py` can hide a dead selector.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (OPS-02, #10):** `crawler.py` now records which extractor won in `meta_data["extractor"]` ("site"/"trafilatura"/"newspaper"/`None`), persisted on `articles.meta_data`; `check_crawlers.py` reports `FALLBACK` (needs attention, non-fatal -- excluded from `UNHEALTHY`/the exit code, included in `NEEDS_ATTENTION`) when every successful sample came from a fallback.
